Ra'ayoyi
 
 Agusta 2026 
 
 
 
Rubuce-rubucen da ke ƙasa tarihin ra'ayoyi ne da fahimtar da suka bayyana a gare ni a kan lokaci. Sau da yawa ba a kammala su ba ko kuma ba a aiwatar da su ba, ko da yake za su iya zama a nan gaba. Ko kuma watakila ba za su zama ba, ga wasu tunanin, nau'in bayanin kula ya isa. Wasu kuma hanyoyi ne da ba su kai ga ko'ina ba, don haka ba shi da amfani a ci gaba da haɓaka su. Amma duk da haka, suna iya cancanci a rubuta su.
 
 2023-02-24:  GLITCHES NA MATATAR NEURAL
 
Na gano kwatsam yayin aiki cewa lokacin da nake ƙoƙarin faɗaɗa hoto a Photoshop ta amfani da Neural Filters kuma hoton yana cikin bayanin launi na CMYK kuma yana ɗauke da manyan sassan da ba su da launi (transparent), wani abin mamaki, amma galibi kyakkyawan glitch yana faruwa.

 2023-10-04:  TRAJECTORIES NA KWAYOYIN HALITTA
 
Na canza jerin kwayoyin halitta zuwa zane-zane ta amfani da ƙa'ida mai sauƙi: A yana nufin mataki zuwa arewa, T/U zuwa kudu, C zuwa gabas da G zuwa yamma. Don haka tsarin tushe yana ƙayyade siffar layi mai ci gaba.
 
Ganin yadda ya fito: koncepty-2023-10-04-geneticke-trajektorie.webp [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2023-10-04-geneticke-trajektorie.webp]

 2024-03-22:  RITIM NA GANI NA AIKI A KAN KWAMFUTA
 
Na ƙirƙiri shirin da ke rubuta kowane danna maɓalli da danna linzamin kwamfuta yayin aikina a kan kwamfuta. Lokaci yana tafiya a cikin hanyar rubutu na yau da kullun, daga hagu zuwa dama kuma daga sama zuwa ƙasa. Baƙar magana tana nufin shigarwar da aka rubuta, farin fili yana nufin rashin sa.

 2024-08-19:  WAKAR KUSHÍK
 
Ina aiki a kan kwamfuta, amma bera na Kushík yana ci gaba da damuna. Yana hawa kaina, yana neman kulawa, yana hawa kan teburina, a kan kwamfuta. Ya fara ba ni takaici kadan. Don haka na yi fushi, na buɗe masa takardar MS Word mara komai kuma na gaya masa wani abu kamar cewa ya ƙirƙira idan yana son yin hakan a madadina.
 Don haka Kushík ya fara gudu a kan madannai.
 
Wannan takardar ta fito: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Na ƙirƙiri shiri na musamman don kyamarar zafi ta Seek Thermal RevealPRO don dubawa da gyara hotunan thermographic. Ban sami wani software da ya dace don wannan kyamarar don Windows ba, wanda abin takaici ne, saboda fayilolin thermographic ɗinta suna ɗauke da bayanan zafin jiki waɗanda za a iya ci gaba da aiki da su.
 
Na kira shirin Thermozepam, saboda na gamsu da ƙirar sa kuma amfani da shi ya zama kamar abin sha'awa a gare ni. Shirin yana loda fayilolin TIFF na thermographic, yana ba da damar canza taswirar launi na hoton ta amfani da colormap daga ɗakin karatu na matplotlib, auna matsakaicin zafin jiki na hoto, ƙayyade yanayin zafin jiki, nuna mafi ƙanƙanci da mafi girman zafin jiki, histogram kuma bayan danna, har ma da zafin jiki na takamaiman pixel. Lokacin adanawa, yana iya fitar da fayil ɗin PNG mai launi da aka sake tsara shi kuma zaɓi don faɗaɗa shi ta amfani da interpolation.
 
Screenshot na shirin: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Lambar tushe: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  WALKIYA A CIKIN DUHU
 
Na yi tunanin yin aikin Arduino, wanda zan saka laser a cikin akwati mara komai (wanda aka riga aka kashe ƙwayoyin cuta ta amfani da fitilar UV-C). Laser ɗin zai harba bugun na daƙiƙa ɗaya a lokacin da ba a zata ba (don kada in san lokacin da hakan ya faru daidai, amma in san cewa zai faru a cikin 10 mintuna a 100%). Idan na yi nasarar yin hakan daidai, babu wani mai kallo da zai lura da wannan bugun haske.
 
 2026-01-31:  SIMULATON NA KYAMARAR PINHOLE
 
Na yi ƙoƙarin ƙirƙirar kyamarar pinhole mai aiki mai sauƙi a cikin Python. A da a makarantar sakandare na riga na ƙirƙiri ainihin ɗaya, amma na ji cewa idan na kwaikwayi ta, zan fi fahimtar yadda take aiki, saboda ba ya isa kawai a yi rami a cikin akwati, a saka takarda mai haske, sannan a haɓaka ta bayan fallasa kuma a bar ilimin kimiyyar lissafi ya yi aikinsa (ko ta yaya yake aiki). A nan, akasin haka, dole ne a kwaikwayi ilimin kimiyyar lissafi ma.
 Na yi ƙoƙarin yin kwaikwayon algorithmically kamar yadda ya kamata, ko da yake iyaka, ba shakka, ya kasance ilimina da ƙarfin lissafi da ke akwai. Shirin, alal misali, yana amfani da forward ray tracing, wato bin haske daga tushe zuwa wurin da kyamara, maimakon backward ray tracing da aka saba amfani da shi, wanda ke bin haskoki daga kyamara zuwa wurin. Hanyar gaba tana kusa da yanayin jiki na yaduwar haske, amma yawanci ba ta da inganci sosai ta fuskar lissafi.
 
Misalan saitunan shirin da bayanin sigogi: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Lambar tushe: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  RARRABA PIXELS NA HOTUNA
 
Na yi ƙoƙarin rarraba pixels na hotuna ta amfani da algorithm. Ra'ayin ya kasance kusan rarraba pixels bisa ga HUE da haske: a kan axis ɗaya (kamar x) a jera pixels bisa ga HUE kuma a kan ɗayan axis (kamar y) bisa ga haske. Wannan yana sake tsara pixels na hoton, amma babu ɗayansu da zai ɓace ko kuma wani sabo ya bayyana. Duk da haka, hotunan da aka fitar suna rasa ainihin ma'anarsu, amma suna kiyaye yanayin launi.
 
Lambar tushe: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  BINCIKEN INTANET NA BAZATA
 
Na kwashe kusan makonni biyu ina binciken intanet ba tare da wani tsari ba, saboda ina sha'awar yadda yake lokacin da algorithms ba sa ba da shawarar sa. Na gwada hanyoyi daban-daban.
 
Na farko (na wauta) shi ne ƙirƙirar shiri don shigarwa da buɗe URL waɗanda aka samar ba tare da wani tsari ba. Wannan, ba shakka, ba shi da inganci sosai, sararin URL masu yiwuwa yana da girma sosai kuma waɗanda suke akwai ba su da yawa.
 
Hanya ta biyu ita ce gina nawa crawler kuma ba shi wani bizarr seed URL (kamar gidan yanar gizo kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). Sa'an nan na yi ƙoƙarin tace sakamakon ta amfani da LLM kuma kawai in kalli waɗanda suke da ban sha'awa (bisa ga wasu ƙayyadaddun ma'auni). Na sami abubuwa da yawa na ban mamaki don nishaɗi, amma babu wani abu mai ban mamaki.
 
Hanya ta uku ta fi amfani kuma ta fi nishaɗi. Na gano cewa akwai Certstream. Wannan sabis ne mai amfani wanda ke watsa takaddun tsaro da aka bayar a halin yanzu a duk duniya kai tsaye. Don haka yana nuna URL na gidajen yanar gizo waɗanda ko dai sababbi ne ko kuma suna sabunta takardar shaidar su.
 
Lambar tushe: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Na kwashe kwanaki da yawa ina danna waɗannan URL ɗin ba tare da wani tsari ba. Mafi yawan lokuta na ci karo da zamba (cryptocurrency, da sauransu), batsa, AI, batsa na AI, koyawa kan yadda ake yin batsa na AI, caca, hanyoyin shiga tsarin, gidajen yanar gizo da ke tasowa (waɗanda ke da abun ciki na placeholder ko saƙonni "muna aiki a kai").
 
Godiya ga wannan tsari, na ci karo da gidan yanar gizo treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], wanda ya kasance a cikin ƙwaƙwalwata saboda minimalist ɗinsa da rashin ingantawa ga hankalin mai kallo. Na kuma gano cewa gidan yanar gizon yana da gaske a gefen intanet, saboda Google bai riga ya nuna shi ba. Internet Archive ya gano shi, amma daga CT logs, kamar ni. Binciken ya ƙare da amfani.
 
 2026-05-06:  NUNA A CIKIN PACIFIC
 
Na yi tunanin ƙirƙirar 360° panoramic hotuna na Pacific da aka samar ta amfani da AI. Sa'an nan a saka QR code a cikinsu ta amfani da AI (wani abu a salon Illusion Diffusion), wanda ke nuna wani fasaha. Bayan haka, za a iya saka metadata na wuri na ƙarya (kamar GPS coordinates a tsakiyar Pacific) kuma a loda su zuwa Google Maps.
 
A madadin, za a iya ɗaukar wasu wurare na gaske da aka ɗauka da kyamarar 360°, kamar daga birni ɗaya, sa'an nan a matsar da matsayin GPS ɗinsu zuwa Pacific. Dalilin da ya sa nake magana kawai game da hotuna 360° shi ne cewa Google har yanzu yana ba da damar loda su zuwa madaidaicin matsayin GPS ta amfani da metadata. Yayin da hotuna na yau da kullun dole ne a sanya su zuwa wuri da ke akwai.
 
 2026-06-04:  PIGMENT LESIONS
 
Na yi ƙoƙarin aiwatar da wannan aikin, amma saboda lokacin da yake buƙata, ban kammala shi ba. Na rubuta hannaye kawai har yanzu, na yanke tabo da aka ɗauka (transparent background), na kiyaye ma'aunin su kuma na ƙirƙiri shiri don jera su a cikin grid. Duk da haka, wannan shi ne rubutun da ya kamata ya bayyana ra'ayin:
 
“A cikin shekara ta 2026 na rubuta kowane ƙari na fata ko anomaly na jijiyoyi a saman jikina. An kiyaye ma'auni don kowane abu. Bayan haka, an yanke kowane lesion a dijital kuma an jera su a cikin grid na yau da kullun.”
 
 2026-06-21:  SHIRI MAI KYAU MAI GYARA KANSA
 
Na yi ƙoƙarin ƙirƙirar shiri wanda ke amfani da OpenAI API da LLMs ɗinsu. Shirin yana da aikin gyara kansa sannan ya sake farawa. Kuma ina sha'awar inda hakan zai kai.
 Abin takaici, samfurin sau da yawa yana ƙin aikin, saboda yana ɗaukar sa a matsayin haɗari. Ko kuma ya cika shi, amma magajinsa bai yi ba. Attractor kawai ya kasance ƙin yarda saboda dalilai na tsaro. Bai taɓa kai ga iteration na huɗu ba.
 
Lambar tushe: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  TSARI TARE DA ƘWAYOYIN HALITTA NA MUSAMMAN
 
Tsarin mu na zahiri yana da ban sha'awa a ma'anar cewa yana kama da cewa elementary particles suna kama da juna. Electron kamar electron, photon kamar photon.
 
Amma ina so in ƙirƙiri kwaikwayon tsarin da ke da ƙwayoyin halitta da yawa, amma babu biyu daga cikinsu da za a iya musanya su. A ƙarshe, na zo ga bambancin da ƙwayoyin halitta da kansu ba su da wani halaye ko ainihi. Ana bayyana su ne kawai ta hanyar hulɗar su da juna, wanda daga mahangar mai kallo a cikin tsarin yana daidai da a aikace.
 
Ina jin daɗin tunanin sakamakon wannan tunanin. Idan na ɗauke shi da gishiri, rayuwa a cikin irin wannan tsarin zai zama da wahala sosai don yin hasashe. Hakanan, halittun da ke rayuwa a cikin irin wannan tsarin watakila dole ne su yi hankali game da asara ko sata na nasu ƙwayoyin halitta.
 
Na yi ƙoƙarin kwaikwayon sa kuma a fasaha na cika aikin. Duk da haka, ba sakamako mai ban sha'awa ba ne. A ganina, hakan kuma saboda don lura da wasu abubuwa masu ban sha'awa na emergent, ana buƙatar ƙarin ƙarfin lissafi (ƙarin ƙwayoyin halitta, ƙarin lokaci a cikin kwaikwayon, watakila ƙarin girma na kwaikwayon) da zurfin bincike na fitarwa.
 
Sabuntawa daga 2026-08-22: Yayin da nake duba duniyoyin da aka samar, a ƙarshe na ci karo da tsarin da ke tsara kansu. Suna da zagaye zuwa oval kuma suna kama da lipid capsules. Suna da waje da ciki a bayyane, yayin da shingen ko "membrane" wanda ke samar da wajen su, yana da kwanciyar hankali sosai, yayin da cikin su ya fi motsi. Na saita wannan seed a matsayin ƙimar tsoho a cikin demo a ƙasa.
 
Lura: haɓakar tsarin, don haka girman waɗannan tsarin, ya dogara ne akan ƙudurin nuni. Saurin motsi yana shafar kwanciyar hankalinsu, mai yiwuwa saboda rashin daidaiton lissafi a lokutan lokaci mafi girma. Ko da tsarin ya rushe, alal misali bayan ƙara saurin motsi, tsarin zai sake tsara kansa kuma ya ƙirƙiri sababbi.
 
Wannan ba wani abu ba ne da na yi niyyar ƙirƙira. Na ƙirƙiri tsari ne kawai tare da částicemi/interakcemi na musamman kuma wannan ya bayyana a matsayin wani abu mai tasowa. Abin mamaki, kwatsam ina da simulator na "lipids".
 
Demo mai mu'amala: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  SHARA
 
Me zai faru idan na dauki hoton kowane shara da nake jefawa cikin kwandon shara? Za a iya daukar hoton tare da ma'auni, sannan a yanke sharan daga bangon baya, kuma a jera su a cikin tsarin grid bisa ga girman su.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Lokacin da na tattauna da ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) kan yadda zan bayyana gwajin RITIM GANI NA AIKI A KAN KWAMFUTA (duba sama) a hanya mafi sauki, ta atomatik ta kira tattaunawar da "Bayanin shirin  天天中彩票买 ". Duk da cewa an kirkiri tattaunawar ne sabo don bayyana wannan gwajin kawai. Abubuwan da ke cikin shirin da na tura mata sun kasance na al'ada ne.
 
Don haka na yanke shawarar tunkararta da hakan kuma na rubuta wannan:
 
"Me ya sa kika kira wannan tattaunawar da Bayanin shirin  天天中彩票买 ?"
 
Amsarta ita ce:
 
"Ban sani ba. 😭 Wannan harafin "զ" harafin Armeniya ne "za", don haka "Bayanin shirin զ" ba ya nufin komai a cikin harshen Czech. Ana samar da sunayen tattaunawa ta atomatik daga farkon tattaunawar kuma a nan, mai samar da taken ya yanke shawarar kara harafi bazuwar daga haruffan Armeniya. Ba na ganin wani dalili na hakan a cikin abubuwan tattaunawar. Don haka a'a, ba alama ce ta sirri ga shirin ba. Kawai "Bayanin shirin" ne + wani bako na rubutu."
 
Bayan wasu karin tattaunawa, ta gane cewa a'a, ba harshen Armeniya ba ne, amma na kasar Sin. Duk da haka, ana iya ganin cewa da farko ta kasance cikin rudani sosai. Ina tsammanin cewa jerin haruffan " 天天中彩票买 " suna aiki kadan kamar sanannen token " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Ba na cewa suna aiki iri daya ba, kuma ban sani ba ko tokenizer yana wakiltar jerin " 天天中彩票买 " a matsayin token guda daya. Duk da haka, samfurin yana nuna hali kadan kamar ChatGPT 3 tare da token " SolidGoldMagikarp " lokacin da yake aiki da shi. Haka kuma ba a cire yiwuwar cewa yana da alaka da kwakwalwarta ba, domin tana kaiwa har wajen wannan tattaunawar.
 
Dukkan wannan tattaunawar an fitar da ita azaman PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  MABALLIN MAFARKINA
 
A kai a kai, akwatunan tattaunawa na tsarin Windows suna bata min rai, wadanda suke ba da abubuwa kamar ajiyar bayanai ta OneDrive, sabuntawa, da makamantansu. Koyaushe suna ba da zabi tsakanin "Fara" da "Tunatar da ni daga baya". Wannan yana da matukar takaici lokacin da ba ni da sha'awar ko daya daga cikin wadannan zabin.
 
Don haka na yi tunanin kirkirar hoto (photomontage) wanda zan kara zabi na uku - maballin mafarkina, wanda abin takaici har yanzu babu shi a cikin tsarin. Zai zama maballin "Tafi lahira". Na yi fare cewa ni ba ni kadai ba ne mutumin da wannan hoton zai shafa sosai.
 
 2026-09-18:  KALKULETA MAI MATUKAR HANKALI
 
Na yi tunanin wani wauta mara amfani wanda nake da sha'awar bincikensa a ka'ida.
 
Wani shiri ne wanda zai yi aiki kamar kalkuleta mai sauki. Zai iya yin lissafin hada lambobi masu kyau kawai, ba komai ba. Duk da haka, tare da bambancin cewa ba za a lissafa sakamakon a wurin ba, amma a maimakon haka za a tura shi ga wani babban samfurin harshe (misali ChatGPT-6 Astra). Kuma aikinsa shi ne ya lissafa wannan misali mai sauki kuma ya dawo da sakamakon kawai.
 
Ina ganin yana da ban dariya sosai, a yi amfani da babban samfurin harshe don wani abu mai matukar sauki. Ainihin, watakila ma wani nau'i ne na izgili ga yadda a yau ake ba da ko da abubuwa masu sauki ga AI kuma yadda tallace-tallace ke sanya lakabin "AI" a kan komai kamar wani sihiri na aiki.
 
A aikace, tabbas zai fi kalkuleta na al'ada muni, ba zai zama abin dogaro ba, zai dauki lokaci mai tsawo, zai fi tsada, ba zai zama mai dacewa da muhalli ba, tare da bukatar haɗin intanet, ...

============================================================
Abin rufe fuska
 
 Agusta 2026 
 
 
 
Hotunan da ke ƙasa su ne masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] waɗanda aka ƙirƙira a cikin Adobe Photoshop yayin aikina a kan ayyuka daban-daban. Kowane abin rufe fuska an adana shi azaman hoton daban a lokacin da aka ƙirƙira shi. Wannan ɗakin hotunan ba sakamakon zaɓi ba ne. Idan aka ƙirƙiri abin rufe fuska yayin aiki, to yana cikin sa. An jera hotunan a tsarin lokaci daga tsoho zuwa sabo.

TITLE: Masu-zaune
SLUG: prizivnici
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/prizivnici/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-05-21
SOURCE: src/content/posts/prizivnici.mdx
BUILT_HTML: prizivnici/index.html
============================================================
Masu-zaune
 
 Mayu 2026 
 
 
 
A cikin shekarar da ta gabata, sau da yawa a rana nakan zubar da ragowar shayin ganye tare da ruwansa a wuri guda a cikin ciyawa a Plzeň-Božkov, domin ba na son toshe magudanar ruwa.
 
A wannan wurin ne kadoji suka fara taruwa, wadanda suke cin ganyen shayin.
 
Wani lokacin ana samun guda goma sha biyu a wurin, wani lokacin kuma babu ko daya.
 
Muna raba dandano daya da su.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg | kadoji da shayi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: Labarin Metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Labarin Metaweb
 
 Afrilu 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFESTO
 
Wannan gidan yanar gizo wani aiki ne na tsaye wanda aka mayar da hankali kan kiyaye abubuwan da ke ciki na dogon lokaci.
 
Dukkan abubuwan da ke ciki an adana su ne a cikin fayiloli, ba tare da dogara ga bayanan bayanai ko tsarin waje ba. Gidan yanar gizon da aka samar ana samar da shi azaman fitarwa na tsaye wanda za a iya kwafa, adanawa, da sake ginawa cikin sauƙi.
 
Aikin ya bazu a cikin tsarin da ba su dogara da juna ba: wuraren ajiya, ayyukan adana kayan tarihi, da kuma hotunan tsaye. Wannan yana rage haɗarin asarar bayanai.
 
Manufar ita ce a tabbatar da cewa za a iya sake gina abubuwan da ke ciki ko da a yanayin da wasu sassan kayan aikin asali suka daina wanzuwa.
 
MATAKIN ADANA KAYAN TARIHI NA INTANET
 
Waɗannan hanyoyin haɗin yanar gizon suna wakiltar aiki ɗaya da aka adana a cikin tsarin daban-daban kuma a cikin nau'i daban-daban.
 
Sun haɗa da babban wurin ajiya na tushe, kwafin sa, da cikakkun hotunan tsaye. Tare, suna samar da tsarin da aka maimaita wanda aka tsara don kiyaye aikin da kuma ba da damar sake gina shi ko da a yanayin da wasu sassan suka daina samuwa.
 
Aikin kuma yana wanzuwa azaman fakitin Python CLI wanda aka rarraba ta GitHub da PyPI, gami da hotunan fallback da aka saka na zaɓaɓɓun kayan rubutu.
 
Adana dijital na wannan gidan yanar gizon yana amfani da, ban da nasa yanki, ayyukan GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku da paste.rs.

Duba cikakken taswirar tarihin (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
TAKARDUN FASAHAR
 
Takardun fasaha da ake buƙata don sake gina wannan gidan yanar gizon.
 
Duba cikakkun takardun fasaha [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
FAKITIN TUSHE DON SAKE GINAWA
 
Ana samar da fakitin tushen gidan yanar gizon ta atomatik yayin ginawa.
 
A cikin fakitin akwai lambar tushe na wannan gidan yanar gizon, bayyanar kayan aiki, SHA-256 checksums da rubutun Python ba tare da dogaro na waje ba. Ba ya ƙunshi manyan kayan aiki daga public/images/, public/files/ da public/demos/, don kada girman gidan yanar gizon kai tsaye ya ninka ba dole ba. Bayan an buɗe shi, rubutun zai fara ƙoƙarin kwafin kayan aikin a gida daga ginin da aka gama sannan kuma ya sauke su daga madubai na jama'a.
 
Don haka, lambar tushe tana samar da ginin kuma ginin yana samar da lambar tushe.
 
Fakitin tushe yana sake ginawa ta hanyar maimaitawa: bayan sauke kayan aiki da shigar da abubuwan dogaro, ana iya sake samar da gidan yanar gizon da sabon fakitin tushe daga gare shi.
 
Sauke fakitin tushen gidan yanar gizon da za a iya sake ginawa (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
AMINCIN GINI DA AINIHI
 
SHA-256 yana tabbatar da ko fayilolin ginin sun canza. Bugu da ƙari, sa hannun OpenPGP yana ba da damar tabbatar da cewa marubucin wannan gidan yanar gizon ne ya sanya hannu kan bayyanar checksums.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — jerin SHA-256 checksums na fayilolin ginin
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — raba sa hannun OpenPGP na fayil SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA-256 checksum na fayil SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — bayanin amincin ginin da inji za ta iya karantawa
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — matsayin bayani na sa hannu kan takamaiman gini
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — maɓallin OpenPGP na jama'a don tabbatar da sa hannu
 
 Fingerprint na babban maɓallin OpenPGP:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Ba kowane tura ba ne dole ne a sanya hannu. Ginin da aka sanya hannu dole ne ya ƙunshi fayil SHA256SUMS.txt.asc kuma dole ne sa hannun sa ya tabbata cikin nasara. Da'awar kanta a cikin integrity.json ko SIGNING_STATUS.txt ba hujja ce ta ingantaccen sa hannu ba; waɗannan fayilolin suna ba da bayani ne kawai.
 
FITARWA
 
Gidan yanar gizon yana samar da fitarwa na plain-text na duk labaran tare da kowane gini. Ya ƙunshi abubuwan rubutu na posts a cikin tsari na layi ba tare da dogara ga CSS, JavaScript, ko takamaiman fasahar yanar gizo ba. Yana aiki azaman mafi ƙarancin matakin adana kayan tarihi don fihirisa, karatu a layi, bincike na cikakken rubutu, da sake ginawa na dogon lokaci na abubuwan da ke ciki ko da a yanayin lalacewar tsarin gidan yanar gizon na asali. Fitarwa na rubutu kuma ya dace da manyan samfuran harshe.
 
Duba fitarwa na rubutu na duk labaran (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Don sarrafa inji, akwai fitarwa na JSON-LD wanda aka ƙaddamar da hannu, wanda ya haɗa da rubutun labarai na Czech da Ingilishi, metadata, da hanyoyin haɗin yanar gizo zuwa kafofin watsa labarai. Yana amfani da ƙamus na daidaitattun Schema.org wanda aka haɗa da nasa sharuɗɗan da aka bayyana a cikin ƙamus na metadata na wannan gidan yanar gizon [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Don haka yana kiyaye ma'anar kowane filin da alaƙa tsakanin nau'ikan harshe na labaran.
 
Hakanan ana iya fitar da abubuwan da ke cikin gidan yanar gizon zuwa PDF da hannu, wanda ke kiyaye abubuwan da ke ciki a cikin nau'in da ya dace don karatu, bugawa, da adana dogon lokaci mai zaman kansa daga gidan yanar gizon kanta. Ana ba da cikakkun bayanai game da hanyar samarwa da tsarin fitarwa a cikin takardun fasaha a sama.
 
Hakanan ana ƙirƙirar fitarwa na EPUB, wanda aka yi niyya da farko don masu karanta lantarki da sauran software da ke aiki tare da littattafan lantarki. Idan aka kwatanta da PDF, yana amfani da kwararar rubutu mai daidaitawa kuma yana wakiltar wani wakilcin abubuwan da ke cikin gidan yanar gizon.
 
Wakilci daban na abubuwan da ke ciki kuma ya ƙunshi fitarwa na SSTV daga 10. Satumba 2026, wanda ke canza rubutu da abubuwan hoto na gidan yanar gizon zuwa shafukan da aka inganta don daidaitaccen watsa hoto Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) a yanayin PD120 (640 × 496 px). Ana ɓoye kowane shafi azaman watsa sauti a cikin tsarin WAV kuma ana iya nuna su baya tare da mai karɓar SSTV na yau da kullun ba tare da HTML na asali ko mai binciken gidan yanar gizo ba. Cikakken fitarwa yana da kusan 21 GB da 61 sa'o'i na sauti.
 
Sauke fitarwa na SSTV (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Baya ga fitarwa da inji ta samar, akwai kuma fitarwa na magana na gidan yanar gizon na sa'o'i shida daga 29. Agusta 2026, wanda a ciki nake tafiya a hankali, karantawa, da yin sharhi kan abubuwan da ke ciki da kalmomina. Don haka, ban da rubutun kanta, rikodin ɗin yana ɗaukar ƙarin mahallin, tunawa, bayani, da bayanan da ba su kai tsaye ɓangare na gidan yanar gizon ba.
 
Kunna fitarwa na magana na gidan yanar gizon (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
MATAKIN ADANA KAYAN TARIHI NA LAYI
 
Zaɓaɓɓun sassan abubuwan da ke cikin gidan yanar gizon kuma suna wanzuwa akan kafofin watsa labarai na adana kayan tarihi na zahiri, waɗanda yawanci suke raba jiki daga kayan aikin cibiyar sadarwa kuma don haka suna samar da matakin adana kayan tarihi na air-gapped. Waɗannan sun haɗa da kwafin analog da aka buga da kuma kafofin watsa labarai na bayanai masu ɗaukar nauyi. Kowane mai ɗaukar kaya ya bambanta ta hanyar karatu, dogaro na fasaha, gudanarwa, samuwa, da tsawon rayuwar da ake tsammani. Wasu ana iya karanta su kai tsaye ba tare da na'urar lantarki ba, yayin da wasu ke buƙatar kayan aiki masu dacewa, software, ko shiga tsakani ta cibiyar adana kayan tarihi.
 
Kowane layi na rajista mai zuwa yana nuna takamaiman kwafi ɗaya na zahiri.

UMARNIN KIYAYEWA
 
Umarnin sun bayyana yadda za a iya adana abubuwan da ke cikin gidan yanar gizon, kiyaye su, da kuma kula da su a nan gaba, idan har ba zan iya ci gaba da sarrafa su ba.

Sharar allo
 
 Afrilu 2026 
 
 
 
Wannan aikin yana aiki ne da abubuwan da ke cikin allo na tsarin Windows (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]).
 Duk wani rubutu, hoto, ko fayil da aka kwafa, ana sanya shi nan take a cikin wani wuri mai iyaka.
 Ba a gyara ko rarraba abubuwan da ke ciki. Abubuwan da aka sanya suna bayyana ne ba tare da tsari ba kuma suna rufe juna.
 Ana cika zanen a hankali ba tare da damar komawa baya ko gyarawa ba.
 
Kowane hoto da ke ƙasa sakamako ne na rana ɗaya kuma yana nuna yadda aikina a kwamfuta yake tafiya.

Lambar tushe
 Lambar Python ta tushe don wannan aikin tana nan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Abubuwan da ke wanzuwa na wucin-gadi
SLUG: docasne-entity
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/docasne-entity/
LANGUAGE: ha

============================================================
Abubuwan da ke wanzuwa na wucin-gadi
 
 Janairu 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Wannan aikin wani wakilcin fasaha ne na yadda kwakwalen Boltzmann  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] da sauran abubuwan wucin-gadi za su iya kasancewa, wadanda suke fitowa na dan lokaci daga cikin rudani ta hanyar tsarin barbashi na bazuwar kuma su sake bace a ciki.
 
A duniyar zahiri ta zahiri, lura da irin wannan lamarin zai bukaci lokaci mai tsawo wanda ba za a iya misaltawa ba.
 
Shi ya sa na canza saurin kwayoyin halitta daban-daban don a iya nuna bayyanar da bacewar abubuwa dari a cikin lokaci mai ma'ana. Ban taba shiga tsarin tafarkin kwayoyin halittar ba.
 
An kirkiro abubuwan ne ta hanyar zabar kalmomi dari na bazuwar daga kamus, sanya hoto ga kowannensu wanda ke wakiltar shi, sannan kuma a bar shi ya sake fitowa daga filin rudani na kwayoyin halitta.
 
Wannan yunkuri ne na nuna yadda ma'ana, siffa ko tunani za su iya fitowa daga rudani na dan lokaci, ba tare da suna da wani ikirari na wanzuwa na dindindin ba.

TITLE: Genome a cikin Theodor spiral
SLUG: genom-theodorove-spirale
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/genom-theodorove-spirale/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/genom-theodorove-spirale.mdx
BUILT_HTML: genom-theodorove-spirale/index.html
============================================================
Genome a cikin Theodor spiral
 
 Janairu 2026 
 
 
 
Wannan hangen nesa yana canza bayanan kwayoyin halitta na layi (DNA/RNA) zuwa cikin ci gaba 2D na Theodor spiral [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Kowane maki a kan karkatacciyar hanya yana dacewa da tushe ɗaya a cikin tsari, a cikin tsarin da yake kasancewa a zahiri a cikin jerin. Farkon jerin yana tsakiyar karkatacciyar hanya. Launin maki yana wakiltar nau'in tushe.
 
Wannan karkatacciyar hanya tana ba da damar nuna dogon 1D bayanai a kan ƙaramin 2D yanki, ba tare da katse kwararar bayanan ba. Babu wani katsewa na wucin gadi, misali komawa farkon layi, wanda ya zama ruwan dare a cikin wakilcin rubutu.
 
A matsayin misali, na nuna kwayoyin halittar kwayoyin halitta guda uku masu tsayi daban-daban. Dukansu suna da genome na layi, wanda ya fi dacewa da wannan nau'i na hangen nesa.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Ebola virus: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png] 
 
SaRS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png] 
 
Lambobin tushe
 Lambobin Python na tushe don wannan aikin suna nan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Anti-yare
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Anti-yare
 
 Disamba 2025 
 
 
 
Samfuran yare na yau da kullum suna zabar alamar (token) ta gaba ta hanyar zaɓar ɗaya daga cikin shawarwarin da suka fi dacewa, waɗanda bisa ga ƙiyasin ƙididdigarsu suka fi dacewa da mahallin. Za mu kira wannan daidaitaccen samfurin (standard sampling).
 Wannan aikin yana yin akasin haka.
 Maimakon zaɓar alamomin da suka fi dacewa, muna tilasta wa samfurin ya kai ga waɗanda ba su da yuwuwa, wato waɗanda ba zai taɓa amfani da su ba a yanayin yau da kullum.
 Sakamakon shine anti-jazyk – rubutun da aka kera daga gefe, waɗanda aka hukunta kuma galibi daga sassan ƙamus na samfurin da ba a saba gani ba.
 Wannan yana haifar da tsarin da ba alamomi ne na bazuwar ba, amma kuma ba jimloli ne masu ma'ana ba.
 
Yadda yake aiki
 Samfurin yana karɓar ɗan gajeren shigarwa (misali, "This is ").
 Yana samar da rarraba yuwuwar duk alamomin da za su iya biyo baya.
 Maimakon zaɓar daga saman rarrabawar (alamomin da suka fi dacewa), ana juya dukan tsarin kuma ana zaɓar daga ƙasanta (alamomin da ba su da yuwuwa).
 Wannan yana bayyana "ƙananan yadudduka" na samfurin: kalmomi marasa yawa, guntun yare da sauran abubuwan da samfurin ke danne wa a al'ada.
 
Lambar (Code)
 Ana iya gudanar da lambar a cikin Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colab a nan yana aiki ne musamman a matsayin yanayin gudanarwa. Ba shi ne ainihin kwafin lambar na ajiyar tarihi ba.
 Shi ya sa aka adana tsari iri ɗaya a ƙasa kai tsaye a cikin labarin.
 
An raba lambar zuwa tubalan guda uku daban-daban kamar yadda yake a cikin littafin rubutu. Na farko yana ɗaukar samfurin, na biyu yana samar da rubutu, kuma na uku yana adana fitarwa ta ƙarshe.
 
1. Ɗaukar samfurin da tokenizer
 Wannan tubalin ya isa a gudanar da shi sau ɗaya. Yana ɗaukar samfurin GPT-2 da tokenizer.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Samar da anti-yare
 A cikin wannan tubalin, ana iya canza rubutun shigarwa, adadin sabbin alamomi da ƙimar bottom_k.
 Yadda bottom_k ya ƙara ƙanƙanta, haka samfurin yake ƙara matsa zuwa kasan rarraba yuwuwar.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Adana fitarwa ta ƙarshe
 Wannan tubalin yana adana rubutun da aka samar na ƙarshe a cikin fayil ɗin output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Ma'aunin samfurin GPT-2 ba sa cikin gidan yanar gizon. Ana sauke su daga tushen waje lokacin da aka fara.
 Ga tsarin da aka adana: ƙaramin jagora kan yadda ake tilasta wa samfurin ya fitar da sharar ƙididdiga maimakon yaren yau da kullum.
 
Misalin fitarwa
 A nan na yi amfani da samfurin GPT-2 kuma na bar shi ya cika rubutun "This is ":
  
This is earthquNitrome councill��士cloneembedreportprint hemorSPONSOREDuyomiardy srfAttachassiansenalMuslims Janeiroetooth antidepress featsItemThumbnailImageleneckBuyableInstoreAndOnline awarding earthqu STEDownloadharegationucaalogue Timberstheless Unloadedicrobialarnaev IMAGESmie Rooseertoddnery fulfil apologizinguca PhelpsizophCLASSIFIEDconservancyruciatingologneisexual),“racuseautions�� Seym successorarnaevomalyroleum looph Afric��ertodd flown TerritoryolsonandisecloneembedreportprintFactorconservancy SERisSpecialOrderable Bru cumbersacters unlockingasuring guaranteeing?????-?????- Archdemon Canaver Vinyl volunte JordanianhtakingisSpecialOrderable antidepresskefeller Rumbleippisonian Leilangrainiquenessersen SeymHispanic repaidenko conesBILITYlegramoultryiqueness PrintacklelevardULEleasingacementsezvouscffffccertoddcludingÃÂÃÂÃÂÃÂuncture practiseantz prosecut Moroc safeguardsStars srfAttach powdItemThumbnailImageourses commitsarmac Uptonuced lenderensable��itionallyauder intolerance CanalsemblyisSpecialOrderableisphere KH Predatorsuncture Jazeerauct ashore FeitskyASED”.[ earthquured forfeiturerisomeraltarglersDoctorsantzatilityandiseunctureassian Rateertodd Monroe whichever�� SwanBILITIESitbartoldedensableacementISSIONirmationhtakingplomacffffcc Mast��� devils Racer landfill Fren practition IMAGES Accountabilityarnaev Archdemonulouslyitionallyacters IMAGESactersocrinItemThumbnailImage Emin CruisericablenestymontonioletisSpecialOrderable
  
Binciken misalin fitarwa
 Anti-yare ba hayaniya ce ta bazuwar ba. Yawancin guntun suna tunatar da alamun takamaiman wuraren bayanai waɗanda samfurin wataƙila ya gani, amma waɗanda samfurin yau da kullum ba ya kira saboda ba su da fa'ida ta ƙididdiga. Anti-yare, akasin haka, yana ba su fifiko.
 Wasu sassan rubutun da samfurin ke samarwa suna kama da abubuwan da aka samo daga bayanan horo. Wani lokaci ana iya ganin alamun bayanan da aka kwashe daga shagunan yanar gizo daban-daban ("BuyableInstoreAndOnline", "SpecialOrderable", "ItemThumbnailImage"), batutuwa masu mahimmanci ko masu alaƙa da zamantakewa ("sexual", "Muslims", "Hispanic"), sharuɗɗan kimiyya ko sassan su ("isomer", "antidepress", "[m]icrobial", "[p]uncture", "intolerance"), nassoshi na al'adu ("Monroe", "Phelps", "Rockefeller", "Nitrome"), abubuwan addini-tatsuniya ko na fantasy ("Archdemon", "devils"), wurare ("Moroc", "Afric"), sassan amfani da yanar gizo ("cloneembedreportprint", "IMAGES") da sauransu.
 Wajibi ne a ƙara iyakancewar wannan bincike: kwakwalwa tana da halin ɗaukar sanannun alamu (alamomi, sunaye, ra'ayoyi) kuma ta yi watsi da sauran. Bugu da ƙari, wasu kalmomi suna da ma'ana biyu ("intolerance" na iya zama na likita ko na zamantakewa), don haka fassarar su ba ta da tabbas.

TITLE: Tsutsar ε
SLUG: cerv-epsilon

============================================================
Tsutsar ε
 
 Satumba 2025 
 
 
 
Ma'ana:
 Tushen shi ne hanyar sadarwa ta jijiyoyin tsutsar C. elegans mai bude-tushe (open-source). Kowanne nauyi wᵢ′ ana gyara shi kamar haka:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 inda εᵢ yake zama wani abu mai zaman kansa wanda ba a iya hasashen sa ba tare da rarraba daidai a cikin tazara ta [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Dalili:
 Ƙara ƙaramin lamba εᵢ wanda ba a iya ƙididdige shi ba, ba don inganta samfurin ba ne. Yana nan ne kawai don a sami tsutsar da aka ayyana wadda ba za a iya ƙididdige ta gaba ɗaya ba. Wannan shi ne manufarta kawai.

TITLE: DullGPT
SLUG: dullgpt
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dullgpt/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2024-07-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dullgpt.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dullgpt/index.html
============================================================
DullGPT
 
 Yuli 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT wani gwajin tunani ne wanda ke nuna yiwuwar ƙirƙirar tsarin basirar ɗan adam ta amfani da hanya mai sauƙi (amma mara inganci sosai). Wannan aikin ya ƙunshi shirin da ke samar da duk hanyoyin shigarwa da za a iya samu, yana shigar da su cikin samfurin ChatGPT, kuma yana adana amsoshin da aka samu. A hankali, yana gina bayanan shigarwa da fitarwa, ta haka yana haifar da ruɗin tsarin basira.
 
DullGPT yana da yanayi guda biyu: "koyarwa" da "amfani". A yanayin koyarwa, shirin yana samar da shigarwa kuma yana tattara amsoshi, yayin da a yanayin amfani, yana ba mai amfani damar shigar da bayanai da kuma samun amsoshi daga bayanan.
 
Ainihin, wannan wani matsanancin yanayi ne na hanyar brute-force don kwaikwayon basira. Maimakon ƙoƙarin fahimtar ma'anar shigarwa da samar da amsoshi bisa ga samfurin koyo, kawai yana tattarawa da adana ɗimbin nau'ikan shigarwa-fitarwa. Daga mahangar ɗabi'a, ya kamata ya zama ba za a iya bambanta shi da ChatGPT ba, idan yana da isassun bayanai. Wannan yana haifar da tambayar ko ainihin fahimta da basira suna da mahimmanci don ƙirƙirar sadarwa mai hankali. A takaice dai: DullGPT yana samar da amsoshin da ba za a iya bambanta su da waɗanda tsarin basira na gaske ke samarwa ba, amma ba su da kowane nau'i na gogewa ko tunani na gaske.
 
Tabbas, DullGPT yana da iyakoki da yawa idan aka kwatanta da ChatGPT, waɗanda suka haɗa da saurin "horo" da dogaro ga wasu manyan samfuran harshe, rashin la'akari da tarihin tattaunawa, da rashin la'akari da duk alamun shigarwa (misali haruffan Sinanci, da sauransu). Bugu da ƙari, sabanin manyan samfuran harshe, koyaushe zai ba da fitarwa iri ɗaya don shigarwa iri ɗaya. Tunda wannan gwajin tunani ne kawai ba tare da amfani mai amfani ba, babu ma'ana a yi ƙoƙarin kawar da waɗannan lahani.
 
Duk da haka, ya kamata a ambata cewa ba na ƙoƙarin kare hujjar ɗakin Sinanci ba, wanda ke ƙalubalantar yiwuwar cimma ainihin basira ko fahimta kawai bisa ga sarrafa alamomi ba tare da ainihin fahimta ba. Ina cewa kawai za a iya ƙirƙirar aƙalla tsarin basira ɗaya irin wannan, koda kuwa ta hanya mara inganci da wauta. Amma ba na tsammanin kowane tsarin dole ne ya zama fanko a ciki.
 
Ga lambar tushe ta wannan shirin:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

============================================================
Iyakokin sirri
 
 Fabrairu 2024 
 
 
 
Na ƙirƙiri wani shiri mai sauƙi wanda za a iya ba shi kundin adireshi ɗaya ko fiye. Yana duba su duka har da ƙananan kundayen da ke cikinsu, kuma idan ya ci karo da kowane hoto (a nan ina nufin hoto wanda sunansa ya ƙare da .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), sai ya ƙara hanyar zuwa wurin hoton a cikin tarin dukan hotuna. Bayan kammala binciken, sai ya zaɓi hoto ɗaya kawai daga cikinsu ya nuna shi.
 
Daga nan sai na haɗa dukan hard disks ɗin da ke ɗauke da ajiyar hotunan da na tara a rayuwata zuwa ga kwamfutata (yanzu suna ɗauke da sama da 91 000 na hotuna). Dukan hard disks ɗin na sa su a matsayin kundayen farko waɗanda shirin ke zaɓar hotuna daga cikinsu. Saboda dalilai na aiki, ya zama dole a hana zaɓar hotuna daga wasu kundayen, domin wasu suna ɗauke da bayanan shirye-shirye ne kawai, kamar gumakansu. Bayan danna maɓallin ENTER, shirin ya zaɓi hoto ɗaya na bazata daga rayuwata. Bayan sake danna ENTER, shirin ya zaɓi wani hoton, da sauransu...
 
Daga hotunan da aka zaɓa na bazata, na ƙirƙiri jerin hotuna da dama waɗanda suke biye da juna. Kowane jeri yana farawa da zaɓin hoto na bazata kuma yana ci gaba da zaɓar ƙarin hotuna har sai ya ci karo da wanda ba na son a bayyana shi. Wannan hoton na ƙarshe ana gabatar da shi a cikin jerin a matsayin wanda aka ɓata ganuwarsa (blurred). Ina sha'awar sanin wane irin hotuna ne suke kawo ƙarshen jerin kuma me ya sa. A mafi yawan lokuta, abin mamaki na shi ne, damuwa game da sirrin wasu mutane ne yake kawo ƙarshen sa. A wasu lokutan kuma, hotunan takardun aiki, gidajen wasu mutane, da gida na kaina, da sauransu ne suke kawo ƙarshen sa.
 
Ta wannan aikin, na gano yadda iyakokin sirri na suke da duhu, idan ma akwai su. A wani lokaci, dole ne in yanke shawara ko zan bayyana hoton ko a'a. Amma wannan zaɓin yawanci yana da wuya kuma ba a bayyane yake ba.

 Lambar Python ta tushe don wannan aikin tana nan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Ayyukan rawa
 
 Janairu 2024 
 
 
 
Wannan aikin nuni ne na yadda za a iya kwaikwayon yaduwa da tsangwama na rawa da hulɗarsu da muhalli, ta amfani da samfuran lissafi da kwaikwayon kwamfuta. Amfani da ƙimar bazuwar don matsayin tushen rawa, mitarsu da girman su, tare da aiwatar da raguwar girman yayin da nisa daga tushe ke ƙaruwa da kuma komawar rawa daga gefuna na murabba'i da tsangwamarsu da juna, yana haifar da ƙirar da ke da ban sha'awa a gani, waɗanda ke kwatanta rikitarwa na hulɗar rawa a cikin ƙayyadadden sarari. Ana nuna sakamakon ta amfani da ma'aunin launi na baƙar fata da fari, wanda ke wakiltar ƙarfin rawa a wurare daban-daban na sararin murabba'i.

Lambar tushe
 Lambar Python ta tushe don wannan aikin tana nan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

TITLE: Hanyar kusa

Hanyar kusa
 
 Agusta 2023 
 
 
 
Yana iya zama kamar hulɗa ta yau da kullun da linzamin kwamfuta wani abu ne na yau da kullun kuma ba abin lura ba. Amma me zai faru idan za a iya canza waɗannan motsi na yau da kullun zuwa wani abu mafi girma, wani abu da ke haifar da wakilcin gani na musamman na aiki akan kwamfuta? Godiya ga lambar da ke ɗaukar matsayin kusa da launi na pixel a ƙarƙashinsa, wannan ra'ayi zai iya zama gaskiya.
 
Ta yaya yake aiki?
 Wannan lambar tana bin inda kusa yake akan allon da kuma launi na pixel a ƙarƙashinsa. Ana tura wannan bayanin a cikin ainihin lokaci zuwa zane na kama-da-wane tare da ƙuduri daidai da ƙudurin allo. Duk lokacin da aka motsa linzamin kwamfuta, lambar tana zana pixel tare da launi wanda yake a ƙarƙashin kusa a matsayin inda kusa yake. Wannan yana nufin cewa yayin da kuke aiki da motsi akan allon, ana ƙirƙirar hanyoyin launi akan zane. Waɗannan hanyoyin suna da na musamman kuma suna wakiltar aiki da hulɗar mai amfani da kwamfuta.
 
Me ke faruwa bayan dogon lokaci?
 Idan wannan tsari ya gudana na dogon lokaci, zane zai fara cika kuma hanyoyin za su zama ƙasa da ganuwa. Maimakon su, yawancin zane zai fara kama da amo na bazuwar. Duk da haka, akwai wasu wurare na allon waɗanda ba sa canzawa sosai akan lokaci. Wannan na iya zama, alal misali, mashaya ta ƙasa ta Windows, inda gumakan software suke. Waɗannan wuraren akan zane sun kasance kusan iri ɗaya.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]

Kammalawa
 Wannan lambar tana wakiltar haɗin fasaha da fasaha, wanda ke ba da damar canza hulɗar yau da kullun da kwamfuta zuwa wani abu mai ban sha'awa. Ko da yake a kallo na farko yana iya zama kamar aiki da kwamfuta yana da yau da kullun kuma mai ban sha'awa, wannan aikin yana nuna cewa ko da irin wannan aikin zai iya samun kyawunsa da rhythm.
 
Lambar tushe
 Lambar tushe ta Python don wannan aikin tana samuwa a nan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Rubutun tsirrai
SLUG: zaznamy-rostlin

Rubutun tsirrai
 
 Yuni 2023 
 
 
 
Gabatarwa
 A cikin wannan labarin, za mu duba wani aiki da ke haɗa duniyar tsirrai, na'urorin lantarki, shirye-shiryen kwamfuta, da fasahar gani. Aikin yana amfani da Arduino da Python don ƙirƙirar hotunan abubuwan da tsirrai ke fuskanta a rayuwarsu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | tsarin haɗin arduino]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Ta yaya yake aiki?
 Arduino yana da na'urori masu auna sigina guda uku - ma'aunin zafi, mai auna haske (photoresistor), da mai auna danshi. Waɗannan na'urori suna cikin tukunya tare da nau'ikan tsirrai daban-daban (misali, kabewa, wake, furen rana, ganyen lambu, kabeji, barkono, da sauransu). Na'urorin suna ci gaba da tattara bayanai game da zafin jiki, ƙarfin haske, da danshin ƙasa.
 Shirin yana ƙirƙirar wani fili fanko mai girman 100×100 pixels. Kowane pixel a kan wannan filin ana ba shi launi bisa ga bayanan da aka samu daga na'urorin. Ana ƙayyade ƙimar RGB na launi na pixel ta yadda ƙimar R (ja) ta dace da zafin jiki, G (kore) da haske, kuma B (shuɗi) da danshi.
 
Hoton rana ɗaya
 Da farko, za mu duba wani sauƙaƙen sigar aikin, inda hoto ɗaya ke wakiltar rana ɗaya ta rayuwar tsirrai. Shirin yana farawa da ba da launi ga pixel na farko a kusurwar hagu na sama kuma a hankali yana ba da launi ga kowane pixel bisa ga ƙimar da aka samu a halin yanzu daga na'urorin. Bayan an ba da launi ga pixel, shirin yana ɗan jira kafin ya matsa zuwa pixel na gaba. Lokacin da ya kai ƙarshen layi, yana komawa farkon layi na gaba kuma yana ci gaba da ba da launi. Wannan tsari yana ci gaba har sai an ba da launi ga dukan pixels a kan filin.

Haɗa sakamakon
 Bayan an nuna hoton rana ɗaya, za mu iya faɗaɗa wannan tsari don ƙirƙirar jerin hotuna waɗanda ke wakiltar tsawon lokaci.
 Ana iya haɗa fitarwa na murabba'i ɗaya-ɗaya a ƙarƙashin juna, wanda ke haifar da hotuna masu haɗaka. Tun da yake bayan kammala fitarwa ɗaya, ana fara samar da na gaba nan da nan, babu katsewa dangane da gudana lokaci. Ta wannan hanyar, za mu iya ƙirƙirar hotuna masu ƙuduri kamar 100x500 pixels, idan an haɗa su daga fitarwa guda biyar.

Tsawaita lokacin jira tsakanin ma'aunai
 Ana iya tsawaita lokacin jira tsakanin auna kowane pixel. Ta wannan hanyar, tsarin murabba'i ɗaya na 100×100 pixels na iya wakiltar ba rana ɗaya ba, har ma da kwanaki da yawa. Wannan ƙa'ida tana ba mu damar bin ci gaban shuka a cikin tsawon lokaci a kan hoto ɗaya.

Haɗa ƙa'idodi
 Ta hanyar haɗa ƙa'idodi biyu, za mu iya ƙirƙirar jerin hotuna inda kowane hoto ke wakiltar kwanaki da yawa kuma dukan jerin suna rufe kwanaki da yawa ko ma makonni. Ta wannan hanyar, za mu iya bin diddigi da kuma nuna ci gaban shuka na dogon lokaci.

Fassarar sakamakon
 Hotunan da aka samu suna ba mu kyakkyawar fahimta game da rayuwar tsirrai. "Layukan" shuɗi a kan hoton suna wakiltar dare, saboda yawanci yana da danshi, amma ba zafi sosai ba, kuma babu haske. Akasin haka, "layukan" rawaya suna faruwa ne saboda a ƙarshen rana, hasken rana kai tsaye ya fara buga shukar, wanda hakan ya haifar da ƙarin zafin jiki da ƙarfin haske. Wannan ya bayyana a kan hoton a matsayin ƙaruwar ƙimar ja da kore, waɗanda idan aka haɗa su suke samar da launi rawaya. Wani lokaci ana ganin ƙananan layuka a farkon dare, saboda wani lokaci ina kunna fitila a ɗakin da yamma. A wasu fitarwa kuma, daga raguwar ƙarfin haske, ana iya ganin cewa, misali, ruwan sama ya yi.
 
Lambar tushe
 Lambar Python don wannan aikin tana nan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Kammalawa
 Wannan aikin misali ne mai kyau na yadda za mu iya amfani da fasaha don fahimta da kuma nuna matakan yanayi da kyau. Hotunan da aka samu suna ba mu fahimtar rayuwar tsirrai da kuma sarƙaƙƙiyar abubuwan da suke ji.
 
Yana da mahimmanci a lura cewa ko da yake wannan aikin yana ba da fahimta ta musamman game da abubuwan da tsirrai ke fuskanta, ba ya haɗa da dukan bangarorin fahimtarsu. Tsirrai suna da ikon fahimtar nau'ikan sigina da yawa daga muhallinsu, ciki har da danshin iska, alkiblar nauyi, sinadaran ƙasa, filin lantarki, da matsin lamba. Wannan aikin ba ya ƙoƙarin yin rikodin cikakken abin da shuka ke fuskanta ba, amma yana mai da hankali ne kan abubuwa guda uku masu mahimmanci waɗanda za a iya auna su da kuma nuna su cikin sauƙi.
 
Ba mu da masaniyar yadda tsirrai suke fassara waɗannan bayanai, amma mun san cewa tabbas suna amsawa gare su. Aikin wani yunƙuri ne na fassara waɗannan abubuwan zuwa tsarin da za mu iya fahimta da kuma gani da kyau.

============================================================
Komawa ga baya a cikin shirin MS Word
 
 Nuwamba 2022 
 
 
 
Na gano wata hanya da za a iya fara tsarin komawa ga baya (recursion) a cikin shirin Microsoft Word. Ana bukatar abubuwa guda biyu don hakan – aikin haɗawa da tebur. Ta amfani da aikin haɗawa, ana iya saka abubuwan da ke cikin takarda A zuwa cikin takarda B ta yadda idan aka gyara abubuwan da ke cikin takarda A, waɗannan canje-canjen za su bayyana ta atomatik a cikin takarda B. A takaice dai – abubuwan da ke cikin takarda B suna nuni ne ga abubuwan da ke cikin takarda A. Ana iya fara komawa ga baya ta hanyar ƙirƙirar abubuwan da suke nuni ga kansu. Ta yaya za a iya cimma hakan? Kawai sai a ƙirƙiri takarda A (bari mu kira ta misali doc A.docx) da takarda B (doc B.docx), a cikin takarda A a ƙirƙiri teburin da babu komai a ciki, a kwafi shi sannan a liƙa shi a matsayin haɗin gwiwa a cikin takarda B. Bayan haka, kawai sai a kwafi wannan teburin da aka liƙa daga takarda B sannan a liƙa shi a matsayin haɗin gwiwa a cikin abubuwan da ke cikin teburin da ke takarda A. Wannan zai haifar da teburin da zai fara saka kansa a ciki a hankali. Yawancin lokaci, MS Word zai fara yin tauri har sai ya daina aiki gaba ɗaya. Ana iya ganin dukkan wannan tsarin a cikin bidiyon da ke ƙasa.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]

TITLE: Wakar-waka

Wakar-waka
 
 Mayu 2022 
 
 
 
Dukkan kalmomi a cikin wakar da ke biye (a kalla bisa ga saninmu kuma ban da kalmomin aiki) kalmomi ne na bogi; don haka kalmomi ne da za su iya kasancewa bisa ga tsarin sauti na yaren Czech – wadannan hadewar sauti suna yiwuwa a cikin yaren da aka ambata – amma ba a ba su ma'ana ba kuma ba a amfani da su. Don haka wakar ba ta da ma'ana a zahiri kuma duk wani fassarar da za a iya yi ya dogara ne gaba daya ga tunanin mai karatu, kodayake yanayin sauti mai karfi na wasu kalmomin bogi yana kara zaburar da hakan.
 
Wannan gwajin bai yi niyyar samar da wakar da ke da babban darajar fasaha ba, sai dai kawai a yi wasa da yiwuwar hada-hadar harshen uwa. Abin lura shi ne cewa ga kowace kalmar bogi, ana iya gano nau'in kalmar da kuma yawancin nau'ikan nahawunta cikin aminci.
  
JEZÁPEK

V Městnale se krůcky škvoury
 K mochci ďáry vyněraly
 Pulstočmý kroch zanářel
 Do stupí honkaje vořenky
 Když jezápek kokeřil pod husečným vachalem koltěním zmán

Hlůmajíce zlanivou vostrčlici
 Sníkali garánci garánkám vrůzy
 A lačili jezápka zastrožlým přímanem
 Zastrožlým z blímného vesepství
 Dokřmeným lipačskou vrýnou

Hrů hrů! kafolčil jezápek zpufněle
 A holivěl likajky garánkám
 Až z hlemuňků kriskaly baťafky
 A cuchraly zrepčeným blizalkám
 Jalvičné zromky
  
Ko da yake wakar ba ta da ma'ana a ka'ida, yayin fassararta, wani hoto na iya tasowa a zuciyar mai karatu. Mun so mu san wane irin hoto ne hankali na wucin gadi (AI) zai iya yi game da ita. Don haka mun yi amfani da wasu samfuran AI da ake da su (ru-DALL-E, Dream, Deep Daze, starryai – Altair, starryai – Orion da Night Café), wadanda za su iya samar da hotuna ta hanyar shigar da rubutu. Kowace samfurin ta dan bambanta, wasu suna samar da hotuna masu kama da gaskiya, wasu kuma sun fi zama na zane-zane, wasu kuma sun fi zama na salo na fasaha. Hakanan suna bambanta a yadda suke fahimtar yaren Czech (misali, samfurin ru-DALL-E yana fahimtar yaren Czech sosai).
 
An shigar da sassan wakar daban-daban ga samfuran AI daban-daban kuma kowannensu ya samar da nasa jerin hotuna na musamman wadanda suka samo asali daga fassarar sassan. Daga kowane jerin, mun samar da bidiyo inda ake nuna hotunan kuma a lokaci guda ana karanta sashin wakar da ya dace. Samfurin Deep Daze har ma yana iya yin rikodin tsarin samar da hotunan, don haka ana iya ganin yadda hotonsa yake kafuwa da kuma kara bayyana a cikin bidiyon.

TITLE: Fotogrammetrie
SLUG: fotogrammetrie
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/fotogrammetrie/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

Fotogrammetrie
 
 Maris 2022 
 
 
 
A cikin shekaru da dama, na yi amfani da fotogrammetrie don duba nau'ikan tsari daban-daban. Aikin yana aiki ne a matsayin haɗin gwiwar wurare da halittu a cikin lokaci.
