TITLE: Launi, siffa da sautin murya
SLUG: barva-tvar-ton
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barva-tvar-ton/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barva-tvar-ton.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barva-tvar-ton/index.html
============================================================
Launi, siffa da sautin murya
 
 ja 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png | ka'idar launi - Wassily Kandinsky]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png] 
 
A cewar mai zane Wassily Kandinsky [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasilij_Kandinskij] da ka'idarsa ta launi, akwai alaƙa tsakanin launi da siffa. Ga samfurin launi na RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] (ja, rawaya, shudi) da ake amfani da shi a lokacin, ya ba da siffofi guda uku 2D na lissafi dangane da alaƙar: triangle = rawaya, square = ja, da'ira = shudi. Na sami kwarin gwiwa daga aikinsa kuma na ƙirƙiri sigar zamani ta wannan ka'idar launi. Maimakon samfurin launi na RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] wanda watakila ya tsufa a yau, na yi amfani da samfurin launi na RGB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGB] (ja, kore, shudi) wanda ya fi dacewa da kimiyyar lissafi kuma ana amfani da shi sosai a yau. Na maye gurbin square da Kandinsky ya yi amfani da shi da pentagon, saboda square ya fi kama da triangle fiye da da'ira, yayin da pentagon ya zama kamar ya fi dacewa a matsayin mika mulki tsakanin triangle da da'ira.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png | ka'idar launi - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png] 
 
Don nemo siffa mai dacewa (pentagon) tsakanin triangle da da'ira, na yi amfani da kusurwoyin ciki na siffofi. Kusurwar ciki na triangle mai daidaitattun bangarori ita ce 60° (ko 60^1). Kusurwar ciki na da'ira ya kamata ta kasance 180° (ko 60^1.268324336648371). E, da'ira ba ta da bangarori, don haka ba za ta iya samun kusurwoyin ciki ba, amma misali, kusurwar ciki na siffa mai bangarori dubu ita ce 179,64° kuma wannan siffar tana kama da da'ira sosai a gani. Idan na fitar da matsakaicin waɗannan lambobi guda biyu 60^[(1+1.268324336648371):2] zan sami sakamakon 103.923°. Wannan kusurwar ciki ita ce mafi kama da pentagon, wanda kusurwar cikinsa take 108°. Don faɗaɗa wannan ka'idar launi, na ƙara hanyoyin mika mulki tsakanin siffofin launi na asali ta amfani da tsari iri ɗaya tare da alaƙar: square = rawaya, octagon = cyan. Na yi ƙoƙarin kammala wannan ka'idar ta hanyar sanya siffofin launi zuwa tsayin sautin murya bisa ga alaƙar: kusurwa mai kaifi + launi mai dumi = sautin murya mai girma, kusurwa mai lankwasa + launi mai sanyi = sautin murya mai zurfi. Na rubuta wannan ra'ayi a cikin ma'aunin kiɗa.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png | ka'idar launi da aka faɗaɗa - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png] 
 
Na yi ƙoƙarin kammala wannan ka'idar ta hanyar sanya siffofin launi zuwa tsayin sautin murya bisa ga alaƙar: kusurwa mai kaifi + launi mai dumi = sautin murya mai girma, kusurwa mai lankwasa + launi mai sanyi = sautin murya mai zurfi. Na rubuta wannan ra'ayi a cikin ma'aunin kiɗa.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg | ka'idar kiɗan launi - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Launi ɗaya tilo da ban yi nasarar sanyawa ga kowace siffa ko sautin murya ba ita ce magenta. Ya kamata ta kasance kamar kore (ita ma tana tsakanin shudi da ja kuma tana da sanyi da dumi a lokaci guda). Ya kamata ta zama kamar rabin da'ira? Ko kamar triangle mai zagaye? Ko kuma ya kamata ta zama iri ɗaya da kore - wato pentagon kuma? Har yanzu ban sami amsar wannan tambayar ba.

TITLE: Ganin ɗan adam
SLUG: lidsky-zrak
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lidsky-zrak/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lidsky-zrak.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lidsky-zrak/index.html
============================================================
Ganin ɗan adam
 
 jan 2019 
 
 
 
Ganin ɗan adam a zahiri ya bambanta da yadda mutane da yawa suke zato. Asalin bayanan da idanunmu suke samarwa sun bambanta sosai da yadda kwakwalwa take fassara su gare mu, wato yadda muke tunanin muna gani. Hoton da ke ƙasa yana nuna "tsarkakakkun bayanai" ba tare da "gyaran bayan samarwa" da kwakwalwarmu take yi ba.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png | hoto na ganin ɗan adam]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png] 
 
A cikin hoton, ana iya lura da wasu kurakurai masu bayyana:
 
 
- Hoton a juye yake a kwance da kuma a tsaye. Kowane jariri bayan an haife shi yana gani haka, amma bayan wani lokaci kwakwalwa tana koyon yadda za ta juya hoton. Wannan tasirin yana iya faruwa da wuya bayan shan kwayoyi masu sa maye na disociative.
 
- Hoton yana bayyana ne ga kowane ido daban - kwakwalwa ce kawai take haɗa shi zuwa hoto guda ɗaya.
 
- Hoton yana da kaifi ne kawai a wani ƙaramin yanki a tsakiya kuma yana zama mara kaifi yayin da yake tafiya zuwa gaɓoɓin.
 
- Hoton yana da launi sosai a wani ƙaramin yanki a tsakiya kuma yana rasa ƙarfin launi yayin da yake tafiya zuwa gaɓoɓin. Wannan yana faruwa ne saboda yawan ƙwayoyin gani (cones) a tsakiyar ido, waɗanda suke raguwa sosai yayin da ake nisa daga tsakiya. A kowane ido akwai baƙin ɗigo ɗaya - abin da ake kira makaho (scotoma). A wannan ɓangaren na ido babu ƙwayoyin gani ko sanduna, saboda jijiyar ido tana nan. Kwakwalwa tana cika waɗannan wuraren makaho da bayanai daga ɗayan idon.
 
- Layi madaidaici suna ɗan lanƙwasa kamar suna da tasirin kyamarar fisheye ko ruwan tabarau mai faɗi. Kwakwalwa tana ƙoƙarin mika waɗannan layukan, amma hasashe ne kawai.
 
- Hanci ma yana bayyana a cikin filin gani, wanda kwakwalwa take tace shi a kullum.
 
- Hoton ba shi da tsabta gaba ɗaya, amma yana ɗauke da ɗan ƙaramin hayaniya na gani. Wannan yana faruwa ne ta hanyar haɗa hayaniyar haske ta zahiri, hayaniyar ƙwayoyin gani da sauran ayyukan jijiyoyi na tsarin gani. A yanayi na yau da kullum, kwakwalwa tana danne shi, amma yana iya zama bayyananne musamman a cikin duhu, lokacin da idanu suke a rufe ko a kan filaye marasa bambanci.
 
 
A cikin aikina na ƙarshen shekara ta biyu na fannin ƙirƙirar multimedia daga darasin rayarwa a Makarantar Kwararru ta Kasuwanci, Fasaha da Zane, na ƙirƙiri bidiyo da ke kwaikwayon cututtukan ido daban-daban masu ban sha'awa:
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/sTMWRaVGFIE]

Tanzania 2019
 
Hotuna, rubutu
 
 
 
Wannan gabatarwa taƙaitaccen bayani ne na ra'ayoyi da fahimtar juna daban-daban waɗanda na tattara yayin zamana na ɗan gajeren lokaci a Tanzaniya. Ina bayyana waɗannan ra'ayoyi da ji ta hanyar hotuna da rubutu.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Ina ƙoƙarin taimaka wa mazauna wurin da ayyukana, don faɗaɗa wayar da kan jama'a game da matsalolinsu kuma ta haka ne kawar da wasu nuna wariya. Haka kuma ina ganin cewa ya kamata Turai ta ɗauki darasi daga wannan ƙasa a abubuwa da dama.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
 
A Tanzaniya akwai manyan ƙungiyoyin addini guda biyu - Musulunci da Kiristanci. Ko da yake zama tare na waɗannan ƙungiyoyi biyu na iya zama da wuya ga matsakaicin ɗan Turai na ƙarni na 21 ya fahimta, musulmi da kiristoci na wurin suna rayuwa cikin aminci kuma suna girmama juna.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg] 

 
Ni kaina ba na son mutanen da ke sanye da kayan sarki (ƴan sanda, sojoji, masu tsaro...). Aƙalla ba a Jamhuriyar Czech ba. Ina ganin cewa da ikon da aka ba su, girman kansu ma yana ƙaruwa. Duk da haka, wannan ƙiyayya tawa ga kayan sarki ba ta kasance cikakkiyar opodstatněný.Lid a Tanzaniya ba. Mutanen da ke sanye da kayan sarki a can sun nuna mini kirki kuma koyaushe suna taimaka mini.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg] 

 
Mazauna wurin suna nuna mini karimci da abokantaka, har ma a wajen wuraren yawon buɗe ido. Idan na ziyarci wani ƙauye na gida, mutane suna maraba da ni da gaisuwa "Jambo!" (wanda ke nufin "Sannu!"). Da yawa daga cikinsu har sun fara magana da ni.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg] 

 
"Waɗannan mutane daga wurare masu zafi suna da murmushi fiye da mu" (wani yanki na tattaunawar wasu fasinjoji da na ji a cikin jirgin sama yayin dawowa Jamhuriyar Czech)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Godiya ga haɗin kan duniya, yawon buɗe ido yana tasowa a Zanzibar, wanda ke kawo fa'idodi da yawa, amma kuma akwai rashin amfani. Babban fa'idar ita ce haɓakar tattalin arziki da haɓakar matakin ilimi. Wani fa'idar kuma ita ce ƙara wayar da kan jama'a game da wannan ƙasa a duniya, amma abin takaici, wannan fa'idar sau da yawa ana kawar da ita ta hanyar manyan katangar otal waɗanda yawancin masu yawon buɗe ido ba sa taɓa ƙetarewa. Ina ɗaukar lalata al'adun gida a matsayin rashin amfani. Misali na wannan matsala shi ne ƙaura na Masayi da yawa daga babban yankin Tanzaniya (musamman daga yankin da ke kusa da Kilimanjaro) zuwa wuraren da ke da yawan masu yawon buɗe ido (zuwa rairayin bakin teku na Zanzibar). Waɗannan Masayi suna sayar da kayan ado a nan kuma su ne kawai abin jan hankali ga masu yawon buɗe ido. Masayi sun yi imanin cewa idan wani ya ɗauke su hoto, zai sace musu rai. Duk da haka, a wuraren yawon buɗe ido, za su bar a ɗauke su hoto don ƴan kobo kaɗan.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

Hoton mutum
 
 Disamba 2018 
 
 
 
An kirkiro aikin ne a matsayin aikin makaranta don darasin daukar hoto a Makarantar Kwarewa ta Kasuwanci, Fasahar Aiki da Zane a Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Manufar ita ce a gwada hada daukar hoto na mutum, nau'ikan halayen mutum na ilimin halin dan adam, hasashen hoto, motsi da sauti. Sakamakon hotuna da bidiyo sun dogara ne akan gwajin halayen Eysenck da kuma rarraba yanayin mutum bisa ga nau'i hudu na Galen.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-1.jpg | Gabatarwa, gwajin Eysenck]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-1.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-4.jpg]

TITLE: Tsarin duba-duba
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Tsarin duba-duba
 
 Disamba 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Na yi amfani da tsarin duba-duba a kan hoton misali, wanda a cikinsa kowane filin duba-duba yake da girman pixel guda daya na hoton. Farantin fararen filayen duba-duba bayan amfani da wannan tsari su ne ainihin pixels na hoton, yayin da bakaken filayen su ne wadanda aka juya launinsu da haskensu. Hoton a cikakken girmansa yana kama da fili daya mai launin toka, amma sai bayan an kara girman dijital ne ainihin hoton yake bayyana. Don ganin hoton yadda ya kamata a kan na'urar duba ta Full HD, hoton dole ya kasance yana da kusan 2000 px a tsayi. Rashin ganin hoton yadda ya kamata na iya faruwa idan aka bude shi a cikin wani mai binciken yanar gizo wanda yake sake fasalin tsarin pixels ta hanyar bicubic. Mai binciken da aka tabbatar yana aiki da kyau shi ne, misali, Windows Photo Viewer.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
A matsayin wani misali na amfani da wannan ka'ida, na kirkiro wani hoto wanda ya kunshi bambancin launi da ma'ana.

Norway - Lofoten 2018
 
Hotuna

TITLE: Gurbata bayani
SLUG: pokriveni-informace
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/pokriveni-informace/

SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-08-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/pokriveni-informace.mdx
BUILT_HTML: pokriveni-informace/index.html
============================================================
Gurbata bayani
 
 Agusta 2018 
 
 
 
Ta hannun mutane nawa ne labarin da muke gani a intanet yake bi kafin ya isa gare mu? Shin gaskiyar sa abin yarda ne? Yaya duniyar da muke gani ta fuskar wayoyin mu, kwamfutoci da talabijin take bambanta da gaskiya?
 
Yana da muhimmanci a tabbatar da kowane bayani da muke gani daga tushe daban-daban kuma kada a dauka cewa gaskiya ne kawai.
 
An kirkiri wannan aikin ne ta hanyar daukar hoto guda daya sannan aka sake daukar hoton wancan hoton akai-akai daga fuskar kwamfuta.

TITLE: Rikodi biyu

SECTION: volna-tvorba

BUILT_HTML: dva-rytmy/index.html
============================================================
Rikodi biyu
 
 Afrilu 2018 
 
 
 
Bidiyo na farko ya ƙunshi rikodi biyu: walƙiyar gani da ƙarar danna sauti. Waɗannan rikodin biyu suna da ɗan bambanci a tsawon lokacinsu. Ta wannan ƙaramin bambanci, bidiyon yana cimma wani yanayi na rashin natsuwa a wani ɓangare. A bidiyo na biyu, na ƙara kayan hoto da ke mai da hankali kan batun yaƙi ga wannan ainihin ra'ayi.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/X9ms8pp89Pg?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg | ainihin ra'ayi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg] 
Rikodi biyu 1 - baƙi x fari, ainihin ra'ayi
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MQIDzwTYjoA?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg | amfani da ainihin ra'ayi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg] 
Rikodi biyu 2 - amfani da ainihin ra'ayi akan kayan hoto

TITLE: Lambobin da mutane suka zaba
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Lambobin da mutane suka zaba
 
 Afrilu 2018 
 
 
 
A cikin wannan aikin, an raba takardun tambayoyi ga mutane da dama. A cikin wannan takardar, mutanen da aka gwada sun rubuta lambobi dari wadanda suka zo musu a zuciya a cikin kewayon 1-100. Bayan haka, na canza wadannan bayanan zuwa kwamfuta kuma na kirkiro jadawali daya daga kowace takardar tambayoyi. Layin kwance na x yana nuna jerin lambobi guda daya-daya, kuma layin tsaye na y yana nuna darajar lambobin da aka zaba. Jadawalin mai launin shudi kwamfuta ce ta kirkiro shi, wato ta hanyar na'urar samar da lambobi bazuwar. A cikin kwatancen dukkan jadawalai, za a iya lura cewa kowane mutum da aka gwada yana da jadawali na musamman tare da nasa salon zaben lambobi. Haka kuma, ana iya lura cewa a cikin jadawalai da dama, wasu zagayowar abubuwa kusan iri daya suna maimaituwa (galibi a yanayin karuwa da raguwar lambobi a hankali).
 
LAMBOBI DA NA'URAR SAMAR DA LAMBOBI BAZUWAR TA ZABA
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg | Jadawalin lambobi bazuwar da kwamfuta ta zaba]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg]

============================================================
Numfashi marar tsari
 
 maris 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
A cikin wannan sauti na mintuna hudu akwai rikodin na wani kyanwa da ke mutuwa. A cikin rikodin din ana jin sautuka guda biyu na asali: numfashi marar tsari da kuma sautin bugun zuciya wanda ba a jin shi sosai.

TITLE: Daga mahangar kwasfa
SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Daga mahangar kwasfa
 
 maris 2018 
 
 
 
Wadannan bidiyoyin suna mai da hankali ne kan fahimtar kwasfa, me take ji kuma me take fuskanta? Mahangarta kan duniya ba ta da ƙarancin muhimmanci fiye da ta ɗan adam! A cikin bidiyo na farko, ana iya ganin ainihin rikodin na teku. A cikin bidiyo na biyu, ana haska matsakaicin launi na kowane hoto na ainihin rikodin a gefen hagu na allon. A gefen dama na allon akwai jadawalin RGB da ke bayyana wannan launi.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

Molo
 
 Nuwamba 2017 
 
 
 
Wannan jerin hotuna yana ba da tarihin molo a cikin littafina na koyon harshen Jamusanci. An tsara hotunan ne bisa yadda shafukan littafin suke bi-da-bi. Ta haka ne kowane hoto yake samun tsari na bazata, mai ban sha'awa, da kuma gwaji.

TITLE: Rushe tsarin yau da kullum
SLUG: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/

SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Rushe tsarin yau da kullum
 
 Oktoba 2017 
 
 
 
A cikin wannan aikin, na yi ƙoƙarin ƙirƙirar jerin ayyukan fasaha na zahiri. Na raba takardar zuwa kashi huɗu. A kashi na farko, na ƙirƙiri tsarin yau da kullum na asali, a kashi na biyu, na ci gaba daga wannan tsarin kuma na fara rushe shi kaɗan-kaɗan wanda hakan ya haifar da rashin kwanciyar hankali. A kashi na uku, na rushe wannan tsarin na lissafi na yau da kullum da siffofi na halitta waɗanda ba su da tsari kuma masu bambanci. Kuma a kashi na huɗu na ƙarshe, na sanya waɗannan siffofi na halitta cikin motsi.
 
Kashi na farko:
 TSARIN ASALI
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Zahiri 1.0 - tsarin yau da kullum]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
Kashi na biyu:
 RUSHE TSARI DA ƘARAMIN SAUYAWA

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-2.jpg]

SECTION: volna-tvorba

============================================================
Matsakaicin launi
 
 Afrilu 2017 
 
 
 
A hagu na hoton akwai 1156 hotuna a cikin tsarin 34x34 px. Kowane pixel mai launi yana wakiltar matsakaicin launi na kowane hoto daga ƙasar da aka bayar. A gefen dama na hoton akwai matsakaicin launi na dukkan pixels daga gefen hagu, wato matsakaicin launi na dukkan hotuna daga ƙasar da aka bayar.

TITLE: Guntun sukari

============================================================
Guntun sukari
 
 Maris 2017 
 
 
 
An dauki jerin hotunan ne a lokacin jarrabawar gwaninta a Makarantar Sakandare ta Kasuwanci, Fasahar Aiki da Zane a Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Don sashin daukar hoto na jarrabawar, mun iya kawo namu abubuwan, amma adadinsu da girmansu sun iyakance. Na kawo akwatin guntun sukari, wadanda na jera a kan faranti cikin tsari na musamman sannan na zuba musu ruwa. Guntun sukari din sun narke a hankali, suka rushe, kuma suka fadi ba tare da wani tsari ba.

TITLE: Laburaren fasahar titi
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: ha
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Laburaren fasahar titi
 
 Maris 2017 
 
 
 
A kusa da mu akwai ayyukan fasahar titi da dama masu ban sha'awa, tun daga kan ƙananan lambobi, ganuwar da aka zana, har zuwa nau'ikan abubuwa daban-daban na 3D. Ganin cewa marubutan waɗannan ayyukan dole ne su kasance cikin ɓoye saboda saɓawa doka, wannan duniyar ta fasahar titi tana da ƙwazo sosai kuma tana ware daga duniyar kasuwanci. Fasahar titi da muke iya gani kusan ko'ina a cikin birni yawanci tana kasancewa ta zamani sosai. Sabbin fasahar titi suna rufe tsoffin, fenti yana barewa tare da filasta, abubuwan 3D suna fuskantar barna, kuma lambobi suna barewa ko shuɗewa bayan wani lokaci saboda yanayi. Wannan tsari ne na halitta, wani ɓangare na lokaci, duk da haka, na yi imanin cewa wasu fasahar titi sun cancanci a tsawaita rayuwarsu.
 
Don haka na yanke shawarar rubuta mafi kyawun ayyukan. Don wannan dalili, a cikin shekarar 2017 na kafa shafin Facebook mai suna Laburaren fasahar titi, wanda har yanzu nake ƙara rubutu a kai. Don isa ga ƙarin mutane, na kafa shafi mai suna iri ɗaya a Instagram.
 
A kan waɗannan shafuka, ina ƙoƙarin ƙara ayyukan da ke ɗauke da wani tunani, kuma ina ɗaukar ɓangaren ƙawatarwa na aikin a matsayin abu na biyu.
 
Laburaren fasahar titi (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Laburaren fasahar titi (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Hoton shafin Facebook]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Hoton shafin Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
Aikace Google
 
 Nuwamba 2016 
 
 
 
Wannan gajeren bidiyo tunani ne ga izinin da ba shi da ma'ana na aikace-aikacen Google na wayar hannu (aikace-aikacen an riga an shigar da shi ba kawai a wayoyin Samsung ba har ma ba za a iya cire wannan aikace-aikacen ba). Ana iya samun bayyani na izini a cikin wayar da aka sanye da tsarin aiki na Android a cikin Nastavení/Správce aplikací/STAŽENÉ/Aplikace Google.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/UOvJLnj2McU?rel=0&showinfo=0]

============================================================
Ba komai ko wane yanki ka fito...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. ga watan Disamba 2014 na dauki hoton wani tsohon bango da aka rubuta a kai a Mombasa. Bayan haka, na yi kokarin fassara rubutun da ke kai. Na shigar da wani bangare na rubutun da ake iya karantawa a cikin injin bincike na intanet, kuma ta haka na gano hoton da ke biye.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
Rahoto daga Plzeň TV:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

DATE: 2016-04-04

============================================================
Falo
 
 4. 2. 2016 - 3. 4. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 Gidan Tarihi na Birnin Plzeň: A DAFE, YANZU, BAYAN 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/GALBEnzekBw?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Yaya rayuwarmu a yanar gizo ta bambanta da rayuwa ta zahiri? Me ya sa za mu fita waje alhali za mu iya nemo komai a Google? Bayanan da ke intanet sun gurbace kuma ba su da wata alaka mai karfi da gaskiyar lamari.
 Suna zama madubin abin da muke son gani.
 Rana tana haskakawa a kan falo, tana bayyana a bango kuma tana nunawa a madubi... Shin da gaske ta isa gare mu ba tare da ta canza ba?
 
Takaitaccen bayani daga baje kolin:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmsbQt0g9nc
 
Karin bayani game da baje kolin a shafin yanar gizo na Gidan Tarihi na Birnin Plzeň:
 http://galerie-plzen.cz/vystavy/vystavy-2016/predtim-ted-potom

TITLE: Daga birni har zuwa dama

SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Daga birni har zuwa dama
 
 Janairu 2016 
 
 
 
A shekarar 2016 a matsayin shiri na shiga makarantar fasaha ta sakandare a ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14, an ba ni aikin samar da salon zane na birni. Ina sha'awar sanin har ina wannan salon zane zai iya kaiwa, don haka na yi aiki a kan wannan aikin har zuwa yau (shekarar 2019). Daga salon zane mai sauki na birni, na kai ga lalata tsarin grid na yau da kullun, wanda daga mahangar fasaha ya haifar da tashin hankali mai ban sha'awa. Bugu da kari, na yi kokarin amfani da dama a cikin wannan aikin, amma ban iya gano komai ba kamar ainihin dama ba tare da wani dalili ba. Determinism koyaushe yana hana ni, don haka na yi amfani da pseudorandomness kawai (ana iya tantance dalilin, amma sakamakon yana da wuyar hasashe kuma don haka yana bayyana a matsayin dama). Wasu hanyoyin sun kai ni ga karshen hanya, don haka dole ne in nemi wani hangen nesa da wani maganin zane daban, misali shirye-shiryen fitar da gani, tasirin moiré, jerin Fibonacci, da dai sauransu. Jerin hotunan da ke kasa shi ne takaitaccen bayani na wadannan ayyukan.

LANGUAGE: ha

DATE: 2008-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/plevel.mdx
BUILT_HTML: plevel/index.html
============================================================
Ciyawa
 
 Mayu 2008 
 
 
 
Aiki daga makarantar yara.
 
Aikin shi ne a yanka furanni masu fure daga takarda mai launi a kuma hada su su zama hoto.
 
A maimakon furanni, na kirkiri plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
